No entanto, o contexto apresenta o arquivo **`GameModeBase.h`** (Documento 1), que declara a classe **`AGameModeBase`**. \n\nCaso sua pergunta se refira à classe `AGameModeBase` declarada em `GameModeBase.h`, aqui estão as suas principais propriedades e métodos documentados:\n\n### Principais Propriedades (UPROPERTYs) de `AGameModeBase`\n*   **`GameSessionClass`","model_used":"gemini-3.5-flash","retrieved_chunks":[{"score":1.8572796761873178e-05,"text":"# AGameModeBase > ## Key UPROPERTYs exposed to Blueprint > ### Class Configuration\n\nThese properties allow you to define which specific classes are used for different aspects of your game without writing C++ code. They appear in the Details panel under \"Classes\".\n\n*   **`GameSessionClass`**: The class responsible for handling online login approval and UI (e.g., `AOnlineSubsystem`). You typically set this via Project Settings or World Settings to match your platform's backend requirements.\n*   **`GameStateClass`**: Defines the actor that replicates game state data (score, round timer) to all clients. This is distinct from the GameMode itself; think of it as the \"Scoreboard\" while the GameMode is the \"Referee\".\n*   **`PlayerControllerClass`** & **`DefaultPawnClass`**: Define what controller and pawn are spawned for standard players. Override these if you need custom controllers (e.g., specific input handling) or pawns that differ from your main character class.\n*   **`SpectatorClass`**: Defines the actor used when a player leaves the game to watch others. Set this in Project Settings usually, but can be overridden here for dynamic spectator modes.\n*   **`HUDClass`**: The HUD displayed by players logging into this specific GameMode instance. Useful if you have different UI requirements per match type (e.g., Deathmatch vs. Team Battle).","title":"AGameModeBase","description":"","source_file":"/data/ue5_engine_root/Source/Runtime/Engine/Classes/GameFramework/GameModeBase.h","chunk_id":1,"ue_version":"5.8","doc_type":"C++ Source API","raw_content":"// Copyright Epic Games, Inc. It governs the game rules, scoring, what actors\r\n * are allowed to exist in this game type, and who may enter the game.\r\n *\r\n * It is only instanced on the server and will never exist on the client. \r\n *\r\n * A GameModeBase actor is instantiated when the level is initialized for gameplay in\r\n * C++ UGameEngine::LoadMap().  \r\n * \r\n * The class of this GameMode actor is determined by (in order) either the URL ?game=xxx, \r\n * the GameMode Override value set in the World Settings, or the DefaultGameMode entry set \r\n * in the game's Project Settings.\r\n *\r\n * @see https://docs.unrealengine.com/latest/INT/Gameplay/Framework/GameMode/index.html\r\n */\r\nUCLASS(config = Game, notplaceable, BlueprintType, Blueprintable, Transient, hideCategories = (Info, list\r\n\t * (i.e. Object.Outer == Actor in the list)\r\n\t * are all automatically moved regardless of whether they're included here\r\n\t * only dynamic actors in the PersistentLevel may be moved (this includes all actors spawned during gameplay)\r\n\t * this is called for both parts of the transition because actors might change while in the middle (e.g. players might join or leave the When overriding this, be sure\r\n\t * checks in PreLogin are also run, and that the GameModePreLoginEvent is broadcast.\r\n\t */\r\n\tENGINE_API virtual void PreLoginAsync(const FString& Options, const FString& Address, const FUniqueNetIdRepl& UniqueId, const FOnPreLoginCompleteDelegate& OnComplete);\r\n\r\n\t/**\r\n\t * Called to login new players by creating a player controller, overridable by the game\r\n\t *\r\n\t * Sets up basic properties of the player (name, unique id, registers with backend, etc) and should not be used to do\r\n\t * more complicated game logic. UGameFeaturesSubsystemSettings > ## Typical usage in C++\n\nIn Unreal Engine, you typically access these settings via the `IGameFeaturesSubsystem` interface or by retrieving the singleton instance of `UGameFeaturesSubsystemSettings`.\n\n1.  **Retrieving Settings:**\n    ```cpp\n    UGameInstance* GameInstance = GetWorld()->GetAuthGameInstance();\n    IGameFeaturesSubsystem* FeaturesSys = Cast<IGameFeaturesSubsystem>(GameInstance->GetSubsystem(UGameFeaturesSubsystem::StaticClass()));\n    \n    // Access the config settings (often done via reflection or direct cast if exposed)\n    UGameFeaturesSubsystemSettings* Settings = ...; \n    \n    bool bIsPluginValid = Settings.IsValidGameFeaturePlugin(TEXT(\"MyCustomFeature.uplugin\"));\n    ```\n\n2.  **Modifying Config at Runtime:**\n    While the properties are marked `config`, modifying them usually requires a restart unless you use specific hotfix mechanisms provided by the framework (e.g., via the Editor's Hotfix system). However, reading these values is standard for initializing feature managers:\n    \n    ```cpp\n    // Check if we need to override default plugin loading behavior based on config\n    TArray<FString> ForcedPlugins = Settings->EnabledPlugins; \n    for(const FString& PluginName : ForcedPlugins) {\n        LoadPlugin(PluginName);\n    }\n    ```","title":"UGameFeaturesSubsystemSettings","description":"","source_file":"/data/ue5_engine_root/Plugins/Runtime/GameFeatures/Source/GameFeatures/Public/GameFeaturesSubsystemSettings.h","chunk_id":5,"ue_version":"5.8","doc_type":"C++ Source API","raw_content":"// Copyright Epic Games, Inc. All Rights Reserved.\r\n\r\n#pragma once\r\n\n\r\n#define UE_API GAMEFEATURES_API\r\n\r\n/** Settings for the Game Features framework */\r\nUCLASS(MinimalAPI, config=Game, defaultconfig, meta = (DisplayName = \"Game Features\"))\r\nclass UGameFeaturesSubsystemSettings : public UDeveloperSettings\r\n{\r\n\tGENERATED_BODY()\r\n\r\npublic:\r\n\tUE_API UGameFeaturesSubsystemSettings();\r\n\r\n\t/** State/Bundle to always load on clients */\r\n\tstatic UE_API const FName LoadStateClient;\r\n\r\n\t/** State/Bundle to always load on dedicated server */\r\n\tstatic UE_API const FName LoadStateServer;\r\n\r\n\t/** Name of a singleton class to spawn as the game feature project policy. If empty, it will spawn the default one (UDefaultGameFeaturesProjectPolicies) */\r\n\tUPROPERTY(config, EditAnywhere, Category=DefaultClasses, meta=(MetaClass=\"/Script/GameFeatures.GameFeaturesProjectPolicies\", DisplayName=\"Game Feature Project Policy Class\", ConfigRestartRequired=true))\r\n\tFSoftClassPath GameFeaturesManagerClassName;\r\n\r\n\t/** List of plugins that are forcibly enabled (e.g., via a hotfix) */\r\n\tUPROPERTY(config, EditAnywhere, Category = GameFeatures)\r\n\tTArray<FString> EnabledPlugins;\r\n\r\n\t/** List of plugins that are forcibly disabled (e.g., via a hotfix) */\r\n\tUPROPERTY(config, EditAnywhere, Category=GameFeatures)\r\n\tTArray<FString> DisabledPlugins;\r\n\r\n\t/** List of metadata (additional keys) to try parsing from the .uplugin to provide to FGameFeaturePluginDetails */\r\n\tUPROPERTY(config, EditAnywhere, Category=GameFeatures)\r\n\tTArray<FString> AdditionalPluginMetadataKeys;\r\n\r\n\t/** \r\n\t  * Returns true if the specified (normalized or full) path is a game feature plugin\r\n\t  * Safe to call from any thread.\r\n\t  */ \r\n\tUE_API bool IsValidGameFeaturePlugin(const FString& PluginDescriptorFilename) const;\r\n\tUE_API bool IsValidGameFeaturePlugin(FStringView PluginDescriptorFilename) const;\r\n\r\n};\r\n\r\n#undef UE_API"},{"score":1.7132766515715048e-05,"text":"# FInterfaceProperty\n\nBased on the code snippet provided, here is a structured summary and analysis of the key components related to **Unreal Engine's Property System**, specifically focusing on property change events, archetype handling, field iteration, and utility functions.","title":"FInterfaceProperty","description":"","source_file":"/data/ue5_engine_root/Source/Runtime/CoreUObject/Public/UObject/UnrealType.h","chunk_id":0,"ue_version":"5.8","doc_type":"C++ Source API","raw_content":"// Copyright Epic Games, Inc. Used to work with array properties in a sensible way.\r\n **/\r\nclass FScriptArrayHelper\r\n{\r\n\tenum EInternal { Internal };\r\n\r\n\ttemplate <typename CallableType>\r\n\tauto WithScriptArray(CallableType&& Callable) const\r\n\t{\r\n\t\tif (!!(ArrayFlags & EArrayPropertyFlags::UsesMemoryImageAllocator))\r\n\t\t{\r\n\t\t\treturn Callable(FreezableArray);\r\n\t\t}\r\n\t\telse\r\n\t\t{\r\n\t\t\treturn Callable(HeapArray);\r\n\t\t}\r\n\t}\r\n\r\npublic:\r\n\t/**\r\n\t *\tConstructor, brings together a property and an instance of the property located in memory\r\n\t *\t@param\tInProperty: the property associated with this memory\r\n\t *\t@param\tInArray: pointer to raw memory that corresponds to this array. This can be NULL, and sometimes is, but in that case almost all operations will crash.\r\n\t**/\r\n\tUE_FORCEINLINE_HINT FScriptArrayHelper(const FArrayProperty* InProperty, const void* InArray)\r\n\t\t: FScriptArrayHelper(Internal, InProperty->Inner, InArray, InProperty->Inner->GetElementSize(), InProperty->Inner->GetMinAlignment(), InProperty->ArrayFlags)\r\n\t{\r\n\t}\r\n\r\n\t/**\r\n\t *\tIndex range check\r\n\t *\t@param\tIndex: Index to check\r\n\t *\t@return true if accessing this element is legal.\r\n\t**/\r\n\tUE_FORCEINLINE_HINT bool IsValidIndex( int32 Index ) const\r\n\t{\r\n\t\treturn Index >= 0 && Index < Num();\r\n\t}\r\n\t/**\r\n\t *\tReturn the number of elements in the array.\r\n\t *\t@return\tThe number of elements in the array.\r\n\t**/\r\n\tinline int32 Num() const\r\n\t{\r\n\t\tint32 Result = WithScriptArray([](auto* Array) { return Array->Num(); });\r\n\t\tcheckSlow(Result >= 0);\r\n\t\treturn Result;\r\n\t}\r\n\t/**\r\n\t *\tReturn the number of elements in the array without validating the state of the array.\r\n\t *\tNeeded to allow reading of the num when the array is 'invalid' during its intrusive unset state.\r\n\t *\t@return\tThe number of elements in the array.\r\n\t**/\r\n\tinline int32 NumUnchecked() const\r\n\t{\r\n\t\tint32 Result = WithScriptArray([](auto* Array) { return Array->NumUnchecked(); });\r\n\t\treturn Result;\r\n\t}\r\n\t/**\r\n\t *\tReturns a uint8 pointer to an element in the array\r\n\t *\t@param\tIndex: index of the item to return a pointer to.\r\n\t *\t@return\tPointer to this element, or NULL if the array is empty\r\n\t**/\r\n\tinline uint8* GetRawPtr(int32 Index = 0)\r\n\t{\r\n\t\tif (!Num())\r\n\t\t{\r\n\t\t\tcheckSlow(!Index);\r\n\t\t\treturn NULL;\r\n\t\t}\r\n\t\tcheckSlow(IsValidIndex(Index)); \r\n\t\treturn (uint8*)WithScriptArray([](auto* Array) { return Array->GetData(); }) + Index * static_cast<size_t>(ElementSize);\r\n\t}\r\n\t/**\r\n\t *\tReturns a uint8 pointer to an element in the array. This call is identical to GetRawPtr and is\r\n\t *  here to provide interface parity with FScriptSetHelper*.\r\n\t *\t@param\tIndex: index of the item to return a pointer to.\r\n\t *\t@return\tPointer to this element, or NULL if the array is empty\r\n\t**/\r\n\tUE_FORCEINLINE_HINT uint8* GetElementPtr(int32 Index = 0)\r\n\t{\r\n\t\treturn GetRawPtr(Index);\r\n\t}\r\n\t/**\r\n\t*\tEmpty the array, then add blank, constructed values to a given size.\r\n\t*\t@param\tCount: the number of items the array will have on completion.\r\n\t**/\r\n\tvoid EmptyAndAddValues(int32 Count)\r\n\t{ \r\n\t\tcheck(Count>=0);\r\n\t\tcheckSlow(Num() >= 0); \r\n\t\tEmptyValues(Count);\r\n\t\tAddValues(Count);\r\n\t}\r\n\t/**\r\n\t*\tEmpty the array, then add uninitialized values to a given size.\r\n\t*\t@param\tCount: the number of items the array will have on completion.\r\n\t**/\r\n\tvoid EmptyAndAddUninitializedValues(int32 Count)\r\n\t{ \r\n\t\tcheck(Count>=0);\r\n\t\tcheckSlow(Num() >= 0); \r\n\t\tEmptyValues(Count);\r\n\t\tAddUninitializedValues(Count);\r\n\t}\r\n\t/**\r\n\t*\tExpand the array, if needed, so that the given index is valid\r\n\t*\t@param\tIndex: index for the item that we want to ensure is valid\r\n\t*\t@return true if expansion was necessary\r\n\t*\tNOTE: This is not a count, it is an INDEX, so the final count will be at least Index+1 this matches the usage.\r\n\t**/\r\n\tbool ExpandForIndex(int32 Index)\r\n\t{ \r\n\t\tcheck(Index>=0);\r\n\t\tcheckSlow(Num() >= 0); \r\n\t\tif (Index >= Num())\r\n\t\t{\r\n\t\t\tAddValues(Index - Num() + 1);\r\n\t\t\treturn true;\r\n\t\t}\r\n\t\treturn false;\r\n\t}\r\n\t/**\r\n\t*\tAdd or remove elements to set the array to a given size.\r\n\t*\t@param\tCount: the number of items the array will have on completion.\r\n\t**/\r\n\tvoid Resize(int32 Count)\r\n\t{\r\n\t\tif (Count < 0)\r\n\t\t{\r\n\t\t\tUE::Core::Private::OnInvalidArrayNum(Count);\r\n\t\t}\r\n\r\n\t\tint32 OldNum = Num();\r\n\t\tif (Count > OldNum)\r\n\t\t{\r\n\t\t\tAddValues(Count - OldNum);\r\n\t\t}\r\n\t\telse if (Count < OldNum)\r\n\t\t{\r\n\t\t\tRemoveValues(Count, OldNum - Count);\r\n\t\t}\r\n\t}\r\n\t/**\r\n\t*\tAdd blank, constructed values to the end of the array.\r\n\t*\t@param\tCount: the number of items to insert.\r\n\t*\t@return\tthe index of the first newly added item.\r\n\t**/\r\n\tint32 AddValues(int32 Count)\r\n\t{ \r\n\t\tconst int32 OldNum = AddUninitializedValues(Count);\t\t\r\n\t\tConstructItems(OldNum, Count);\r\n\t\treturn OldNum;\r\n\t}\r\n\t/**\r\n\t*\tAdd a blank, constructed values to the end of the array.\r\n\t*\t@return\tthe index of the newly added item.\r\n\t**/\r\n\tUE_FORCEINLINE_HINT int32 AddValue()\r\n\t{ \r\n\t\treturn AddValues(1);\r\n\t}\r\n\t/**\r\n\t*\tAdd uninitialized values to the end of the array.\r\n\t*\t@param\tCount: the number of items to insert.\r\n\t*\t@return\tthe index of the first newly added item.\r\n\t**/\r\n\tint32 AddUninitializedValues(int32 Count)\r\n\t{\r\n\t\tcheck(Count>=0);\r\n\t\tcheckSlow(Num() >= 0);\r\n\t\tconst int32 OldNum = WithScriptArray([this, Count](auto* Array) { return Array->Add(Count, ElementSize, ElementAlignment); });\r\n\t\treturn OldNum;\r\n\t}\r\n\t/**\r\n\t*\tAdd an uninitialized value to the end of the array.\r\n\t*\t@return\tthe index of the newly added item.\r\n\t**/\r\n\tUE_FORCEINLINE_HINT int32 AddUninitializedValue()\r\n\t{\r\n\t\treturn AddUninitializedValues(1);\r\n\t}\r\n\t/**\r\n\t *\tInsert blank, constructed values into the array.\r\n\t *\t@param\tIndex: index of the first inserted item after completion\r\n\t *\t@param\tCount: the number of items to insert.\r\n\t**/\r\n\tvoid InsertValues( int32 Index, int32 Count = 1)\r\n\t{\r\n\t\tcheck(Count>=0);\r\n\t\tcheck(Index>=0 && Index <= Num());\r\n\t\tWithScriptArray([this, Index, Count](auto* Array) { Array->Insert(Index, Count, ElementSize, ElementAlignment); });\r\n\t\tConstructItems(Index, Count);\r\n\t}\r\n\t/**\r\n\t *\tRemove all values from the array, calling destructors, etc as appropriate.\r\n\t *\t@param Slack: used to presize the array for a subsequent add, to avoid reallocation.\r\n\t**/\r\n\tvoid EmptyValues(int32 Slack = 0)\r\n\t{\r\n\t\tcheckSlow(Slack>=0);\r\n\t\tconst int32 OldNum = NumUnchecked();\r\n\t\tif (OldNum)\r\n\t\t{\r\n\t\t\tDestructItems(0, OldNum);\r\n\t\t}\r\n\t\tif (OldNum || Slack)\r\n\t\t{\r\n\t\t\tWithScriptArray([this, Slack](auto* Array) { Array->Empty(Slack, ElementSize, ElementAlignment); });\r\n\t\t}\r\n\t}\r\n\t/**\r\n\t *\tRemove values from the array, calling destructors, etc as appropriate.\r\n\t *\t@param Index: first item to remove.\r\n\t *\t@param Count: number of items to remove.\r\n\t**/\r\n\tvoid RemoveValues(int32 Index, int32 Count = 1)\r\n\t{\r\n\t\tcheck(Count>=0);\r\n\t\tcheck(Index>=0 && Index + Count <= Num());\r\n\t\tDestructItems(Index, Count);\r\n\t\tWithScriptArray([this, Index, Count](auto* Array) { Array->Remove(Index, Count, ElementSize, ElementAlignment); });\r\n\t}\r\n\r\n\t/**\r\n\t*\tClear values in the array. The meaning of clear is defined by the property system.\r\n\t*\t@param Index: first item to clear.\r\n\t*\t@param Count: number of items to clear.\r\n\t**/\r\n\tvoid ClearValues(int32 Index, int32 Count = 1)\r\n\t{\r\n\t\tcheck(Count>=0);\r\n\t\tcheck(Index>=0);\r\n\t\tClearItems(Index, Count);\r\n\t}\r\n\r\n\t/**\r\n\t *\tSwap two elements in the array, does not call constructors and destructors\r\n\t *\t@param A index of one item to swap.\r\n\t *\t@param B index of the other item to swap.\r\n\t**/\r\n\tvoid SwapValues(int32 A, int32 B)\r\n\t{\r\n\t\tWithScriptArray([this, A, B](auto* Array) { Array->SwapMemory(A, B, ElementSize); });\r\n\t}\r\n\r\n\t/**\r\n\t *\tMove the allocation from another array and make it our own.\r\n\t *\t@note The arrays MUST be of the same type, and this function will NOT validate that!\r\n\t *\t@param InOtherArray The array to move the allocation from.\r\n\t**/\r\n\tvoid MoveAssign(void* InOtherArray)\r\n\t{\r\n\t\tcheckSlow(InOtherArray);\r\n\t\t// FScriptArray::MoveAssign does not call destructors for our elements, so do that before calling it.\r\n\t\tDestructItems(0, GetActiveNode() const;\r\n\r\n\t/**\r\n\t * Returns the node corresponding to the currently active property, or if the currently active property\r\n\t * is not a member variable (i.e. inside of a const;\r\n\r\nprotected:\r\n\t/**\r\n\t * In a hierarchy of properties being edited, corresponds to the property that is currently\r\n\t * being processed by Pre/PostEditChange\r\n\t */\r\n\tTDoubleLinkedListNode* ActivePropertyNode;\r\n\r\n\t/**\r\n\t * In a hierarchy of properties being edited, corresponds to the class member property which\r\n\t * contains the property that is currently being processed by Pre/PostEditChange. Add new enums to add new functionality.\r\n\tinline const Type Unspecified\t\t= 1 << 0;\r\n\t//Array Add\r\n\tinline const Type ArrayAdd\t\t\t= 1 << 1;\r\n\t//Array Remove\r\n\tinline const Type ArrayRemove\t\t= 1 << 2;\r\n\t//Array Clear\r\n\tinline const Type ArrayClear\t\t= 1 << 3;\r\n\t//Value Set\r\n\tinline const Type ValueSet\t\t\t= 1 << 4;\r\n\t//Duplicate\r\n\tinline const Type Duplicate\t\t\t= 1 << 5;\r\n\t//Interactive, e.g. dragging a slider. }\r\n\r\n\t/**\r\n\t * Gets an object being edited by this change event. Call GetNumObjectsBeingEdited first to check if there are valid objects\r\n\t * @return The object being edited or nullptr if no object was found\r\n\t */\r\n\tconst UObject* GetObjectBeingEdited(int32 Index) const { return TopLevelObjects[Index]; }\r\n\r\n\t/**\r\n\t * Simple utility to get the name of the property and takes care of the possible null property.\r\n\t */\r\n\tFName GetPropertyName() const\r\n\t{\r\n\t\treturn (Property != nullptr) ? Property->GetFName() : NAME_None;\r\n\t}\r\n\r\n\t/**\r\n\t * Simple utility to get the name of the object's member property and takes care of the possible null property.\r\n\t */\r\n\tFName GetMemberPropertyName() const\r\n\t{\r\n\t\treturn (MemberProperty != nullptr) ? MemberProperty->GetFName() : NAME_None;\r\n\t}\r\n\r\n\t/**\r\n\t * The actual property that changed\r\n\t */\r\n\tFProperty* Property;\r\n\r\n\t/**\r\n\t * The member property of the object that PostEditChange is being called on.  \r\n\t * For example if the property that changed is inside a struct on the object, this property is the struct property\r\n\t */\r\n\tFProperty* MemberProperty;\r\n\r\n\t// The kind of change event that occurred\r\n\tEPropertyChangeType::Type ChangeType;\r\n\r\n\t// Used by the param system to say which object is receiving the event in the case of multi-select\r\n\tint32 ObjectIteratorIndex;\r\nprivate:\r\n\t//In the property window, multiple objects can be selected at once. unless a set of changed instances is provided.\r\n\tbool bFilterChangedInstances;\r\n\r\n\t/** List of top level objects being changed */\r\n\tTArrayView<const UObject* const>
